## Semantic Chunks
### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`
- **Hash**: `3c70796d2c73108752e583989ea8a858b985f1ad72d59ef78ce4cb58ac7a4f69`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-teachers-tell-when-a-student-pasted-text-into-an-essay` (Similarity: 0.9580)

```text
Yes. Teachers using modern writing analytics and document audit logs can immediately tell if an essay was drafted in an external application and pasted into the assigned LMS document.

When an essay is composed directly in an integrated document editor, the creation timeline shows hours of continuous keystrokes, typing pauses, backspaces, and paragraph reorganizations. In contrast, when an essay is composed in an external application—whether in Microsoft Word, Apple Pages, a personal Google account, or generated by ChatGPT—the assigned document remains completely blank until a single, massive paste event populates the entire text in a fraction of a second.

Through Checkmark Plagiarism's External Paste Detection , educators receive instant visual summaries of external paste events, keystroke-to-paste ratios, and total active drafting durations.

Checkmark Plagiarism supports external paste verification by pairing essay writing playback with AI detection , plagiarism detection , autograding , and integrations with Canvas and Google Classroom .
```

### Section: The 4 Forensic Clues of an Externally Drafted Essay
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `8014f654997593ac04f358b9139f117de33db4b6ef359a54fd42100258bf82b5`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-teachers-tell-if-students-compose-their-work-somewhere-else-first` (Similarity: 0.8679)

```text
1. Zero Incremental Keystroke History

The assigned LMS document has 0 keystrokes recorded for thesis formulation, outline expansion, or paragraph drafting.

2. 100% Paste-to-Keystroke Ratio

Checkmark's dashboard flags that 100% of the document characters were inserted via external clipboard paste rather than manual typing.

3. Minimal Active Document Duration

The total active editing session on the LMS lasts only 2–3 minutes (just long enough to paste, format the title, and click Submit).

4. External Formatting Artifacts

Pasting from external software often carries subtle HTML artifacts, non-standard line breaks, or font family shifts embedded in the payload.
```

### Section: How to Differentiate Legitimate Offline Writing from AI Cheating
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `4d3bf276f808e2af44426bdf0e9eee869504b6b563a166b3c96f4457f48772b9`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/my-students-essay-appeared-all-at-once-did-they-use-ai` (Similarity: 0.9091)

```text
When an essay is pasted from an external source, how can a teacher verify whether the student genuinely wrote it in Microsoft Word or generated it with ChatGPT?

Legitimate Offline Human Writing

Student can produce original .docx file with creation metadata. 
 Word document contains multi-day save history and revisions. 
 Citations point to real academic sources in library databases. 
 Student fluently explains vocabulary and arguments in a conference.

Unauthorized External AI Paste

Student has no intermediate drafts, notes, or previous files. 
 Checkmark AI detection flags 95%+ probability on pasted payload. 
 Citations contain hallucinated authors or non-existent DOIs. 
 Student struggles to define complex vocabulary orally.

Read more in how Checkmark writing process analysis works .
```

### Section: Guidelines for Students Who Prefer Drafting in Word
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `73a18fb25aea831360d4c267985ae242869b47c5aebfb59aeecd3497ab54d8b0`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/should-students-be-required-to-submit-drafts` (Similarity: 0.8977)

```text
If students prefer to draft offline, provide clear instructions to protect their academic standing:

Keep Version History Active: Enable AutoSave and revision tracking in Microsoft Word or Apple Pages. 
 Save Milestone Files: Save drafts as Draft_v1.docx , Draft_v2.docx , and Final_Essay.docx across multiple days. 
 Transfer Early: Paste rough outlines into the LMS editor during early checkpoints and complete final polishing within the platform.
```

### Section: A 5-Step Educator Verification Protocol
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `85b3a23a447249df41e6c045f82a93c2b1d14f65787971d86818a1b0afb2c329`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-teachers-tell-if-students-compose-their-work-somewhere-else-first` (Similarity: 0.9149)

```text
Educator External Paste Audit Checklist:

1. Check the Checkmark writing playback summary in SpeedGrader for active typing duration. 
 2. Note whether the entire submission consists of a single external paste event. 
 3. Inspect the Original Preserved Paste payload for AI language markers and fake citations. 
 4. If the student claims offline drafting, request the original external file with metadata. 
 5. Hold a 2-minute oral check-in to confirm spoken alignment with the submitted text.
```
